<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me design C2 历史仪表盘. Per the design review: new `packages/shared/src/overview.ts` (pure aggregation: session count, verdict distribution totals, most evidence-lacking category, average probe count, last 5 session trend points), `GET /api/sessions/overview`, history bar top block; only show trend when ≥2 sessions.

But wait — now that C1 exists, the overview can be richer: version chains. Let's think about what's genuinely valuable and implementable.

Key design considerations:
1. Aggregation across sessions. The tricky part: sessions have different resumes! Aggregating verdict counts across different resumes is questionable. But for a personal tool, the user runs 1-3 resumes. Hmm, but the honest approach: group by resumeHash? Actually the roadmap says "成长档案（跨场次能力曲线）: 同一份简历反复跑，看每条主张的判定怎么变、哪个类别总被打回".

So C2 = 历史仪表盘: aggregate over sessions. The honest framing: aggregate over *all* sessions but show the filters. Or better: aggregate per "resume family"? Hmm.

Let me keep it clean and honest:
- overview aggregates over sessions (those with judgments), showing: 场次数、判定分布合计、最常欠证据的类别、平均追问数、最近 N 场趋势。
- The key honesty issue: 跨场次聚合的前提是这些场次在说同一件事。Different resumes → aggregation meaningless. So I should compute a `resumeGroups` or at least report how many distinct resume hashes are involved, and default to aggregating... hmm.

Actually a simpler, defensible approach: aggregate across all sessions, but each trend point carries its own resumeHash, and if there are multiple distinct resumes, show a note "这些场次来自 N 份不同的简历，趋势只在同一份简历上可比". Even better: filter to the same resumeHash as the latest session by default? That's complex UI.

Let me think about what actually demonstrates value in a 5-8min demo AND is honest. 

I think the best design:
- `overview(entries)`: entries are per-session snapshots `{id, jobTitle, createdAt, resumeHash, status, claims: JudgedClaimLike[], probes}`.
- Output: 
  - `sessions`: total, judged (with claims), distinctResumes
  - `verdicts`: VerdictCounts aggregate
  - `categories`: CategoryShortfall[] aggregate (复用 categoryShortfalls)
  - `probes`: avg per judged session, min/max
  - `trend`: 最近 N 场 (default 5) 的 {id, jobTitle, createdAt, solid, judged, weak, probes, resumeHash} — 按 createdAt 升序
  - `headline`: one sentence
  - `resumeChanged` / `mixedResumes: boolean`

And the "version chain" part: C1 gives us parent_session_id. The dashboard could show the chain count.

Hmm, but there's a subtlety: aggregating verdict distributions across different resumes is the main risk. I'll surface `distinctResumes` and, when >1, add a caveat sentence in the headline... Actually cleaner: the dashboard aggregates **by resume**: groups keyed by resumeHash, sorted by latest first, each group showing its own verdict distribution + trend. That's honest and more useful: "同一份简历反复跑，看趋势". And it naturally handles multiple jobs.

But that complicates the UI. Let's see: 历史栏 top block. IntakePanel history list is already a narrow column (330px). A dashboard there must be compact.

Decision: aggregate **globally** but expose `byResume` groups, and the UI shows the selected/active resume group's trend (or the latest group). Hmm, over-engineering.

Simpler and still honest: 
- shared `overview.ts` computes over a list of session snapshots.
- Core aggregate: totals + verdict counts + category shortfalls + probes + trend points.
- `mixedResumes` boolean + `distinctResumes` count, and headline includes a caveat when mixed: "这些场次来自 N 份不同的简历，判定分布混在一起看只能当参考；趋势线只在同一份简历上才有意义。"

Wait, "趋势线只在同一份简历上才有意义" — but the trend includes resumeHash per point and UI can mark points where the resume changed. Good.

Hmm, actually let me reconsider: is global aggregation even what a user wants? The user runs their resume against job A, then job B. Verdict distribution across both is meaningful-ish ("我的简历整体站得住率"). Category shortfalls across both is actually *more* meaningful (技能类总被打回 = 简历本身的问题). So global aggregation is defensible, with the caveat. Good.

Now the "comparable" question: should probes-average count sessions without probes? No — average over judged sessions only, and report min/max.

Now the important design point to write in docs/ADR: **仪表盘只聚合并存的判定，不重算判定**. And **不引入新表**：所有数据从现表读（session/claim/probe）。Actually for performance, we need to read claims for each session — that's N queries. Fine for tens of sessions. listSessions(limit=50) default.

Hmm, but "avg 追问数" needs probes count per session: `SELECT session_id, COUNT(*) FROM probe GROUP BY session_id` — one query better than N. Let me add a repo function `probeCounts(db)` returning Record<sessionId, number>. And claims: we need verdict distribution per session → could do one aggregate query.
```ts
/**
 * 跨场次的历史仪表盘（P11 · C2）。
 *
 * 单场报告回答「这一版哪里站不住」；这一层回答「我一共跑了几场、整体在往哪走」。
 * 它只做聚合，不重新判任何一条主张：判定是什么，合计出来就是什么。
 * 判定分布与类别短板直接复用 compare.ts 与 shortfall.ts 的纯函数——
 * 同一条结论在两个地方各算一遍，迟早会给出两个答案（D47 / F63 同族）。
 *
 * 全是纯函数、同样输入同样输出（报告与界面共用这一份，回放也依赖它稳定）。
 */
import type { VerdictKind } from './domain.ts';
import { VERDICT_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
import type { JudgedClaimLike, CategoryShortfall } from './shortfall.ts';
import { categoryShortfalls, categoryLine } from './shortfall.ts';
import { verdictCounts } from './compare.ts';

export interface OverviewRun {
  id: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  createdAt: string;
  claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[];
  probes: number;
  parentSessionId?: string | null;
}

export const OVERVIEW_TREND_DEFAULT = 5;

export interface TrendPoint { ... }

export interface HistoryOverview { ... }

export function overviewOf(runs, options = {}): HistoryOverview
```

Implementation:

```ts
export function overviewOf(
  runs: readonly OverviewRun[],
  options: { trendLimit?: number } = {},
): HistoryOverview {
  const ordered = [...runs].sort(compareRuns2);
  const allClaims = ordered.flatMap(r => r.claims);  // careful: flatMap exists
  const counts = verdictCounts(allClaims);
  const categories = categoryShortfalls(allClaims);
  const trendLimit = Math.max(0, options.trendLimit ?? OVERVIEW_TREND_DEFAULT);
  const trendSource = trendLimit === 0 ? [] : ordered.slice(Math.max(0, ordered.length - trendLimit));
  const trend = trendSource.map(trendPointOf);
  const probesTotal = ordered.reduce((sum, run) => sum + run.probes, 0);
  const body = {
    sessions: ordered.length,
    judged: counts.judged,
    solid: counts.solid,
    weak: counts.weak,
    counts: counts.counts,
    categories,
    probesTotal,
    averageProbes: ordered.length === 0 ? null : round1(probesTotal / ordered.length),
    trend,
  };
  return { ...body, headline: overviewHeadline(body) };
}
```

Note: `flatMap` on readonly array — fine in ES2019+.

`compareRuns2` name: use `byCreatedAt`.

```ts
function byCreatedAt(a: OverviewRun, b: OverviewRun): number {
  if (a.createdAt !== b.createdAt) return a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? -1 : 1;
  return a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0;
}
```

round1: Math.round(x * 10) / 10.

trendPointOf:
```ts
function trendPointOf(run: OverviewRun): TrendPoint {
  const counts = verdictCounts(run.claims);
  return {
    sessionId: run.id,
    jobTitle: run.jobTitle,
    createdAt: run.createdAt,
    judged: counts.judged,
    solid: counts.solid,
    solidRate: counts.judged === 0 ? null : Math.round((counts.solid / counts.judged) * 100) / 100,
    probes: run.probes,
  };
}
```

overviewHeadline:
```ts
export function overviewHeadline(plan: Omit<HistoryOverview, 'headline'>): string {
  if (plan.sessions === 0) return '还没有跑过任何一场。跑完第一场，这里会给出跨场次的判定分布。';
  if (plan.judged === 0) return '已有 ' + String(plan.sessions) + ' 场，但一条判定都还没有跑出来。';
  const rate = Math.round((plan.solid / plan.judged) * 100);
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push('共 ' + String(plan.sessions) + ' 场、已判 ' + String(plan.judged) + ' 条主张，站得住 ' + String(plan.solid) + ' 条（' + String(rate) + '%）');
  const worst = plan.categories.filter(c => c.weak > 0)[0];
  if (worst !== undefined) parts.push('最常欠证据的是' + categoryLine(worst));
  if (plan.averageProbes !== null) parts.push('平均每场被追问 ' + String(plan.averageProbes) + ' 次');
  const first = plan.trend[0];
  const last = plan.trend[plan.trend.length - 1];
  if (first !== undefined && last !== undefined && plan.trend.length >= 2 && first.sessionId !== last.sessionId) {
    parts.push('最近 ' + String(plan.trend.length) + ' 场的站得住 ' + solidPhrase(first) + ' → ' + solidPhrase(last));
  }
  return parts.join('；') + '。';
}
```

Hmm categoryLine gives "技能掌握 · 欠 4 条（证据缺失 3 · 表述过度 1）" — embedding in "最常欠证据的是技能掌握 · 欠 4 条（...）" reads awkward. Better: use item.label + ' ' + item.weak + ' 条'. Let me do 「最常欠证据的是「技能掌握」（4 条）」.

solidPhrase(point) = judged === 0 ? '未判定' : solid + '/' + judged 条. Let's write: `'最近 5 场的站得住 1/5 → 4/5'`.

Let me finalize with cleaner wording:
- parts.push('最近 ' + n + ' 场站得住的条数 ' + first.solid + '/' + first.judged + ' → ' + last.solid + '/' + last.judged)

Now the server side: `packages/server/src/overview.ts` with `historyOverview(db, limit)`:
```ts
import type { Db } from './store/db.ts';
import { listSessions, listClaims, listProbes } from './store/index.ts';
import { overviewOf, type HistoryOverview, type OverviewRun } from '@crossexam/shared';

export function historyOverview(db: Db, limit = 50): HistoryOverview {
  const sessions = listSessions(db, limit);
  const runs: OverviewRun[] = sessions.map(s => ({
    id: s.id, jobTitle: s.jobTitle, createdAt: s.createdAt,
    claims: listClaims(db, s.id).map(c => ({ id: c.id, text: c.text, category: c.category, verdict: c.verdict })),
    probes: listProbes(db, s.id).length,
    parentSessionId: s.parentSessionId,
  }));
  return overviewOf(runs);
}
```

That's N+1 queries but limit is 50 and it's sqlite local — fine. Could note it.

Design of overview.ts:

```ts
export interface RunRecord {
  id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[];
  probes?: number | null;
}

export interface OverviewPoint {
  id: string; createdAt: string; jobTitle: string;
  total, judged, solid, weak, solidRate (0-100), probes: number|null
}

export interface CategoryShare { category, label, total, judged, solid, weak, counts, /** 出现过的场次数 */ runs }
```

Let me add to repo.ts:
```ts
export function countProbes(db: Db, sessionId: string): number {
  const row = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM probe WHERE session_id = ?').get(sessionId) as { n: number } | undefined;
  return row === undefined ? 0 : row.n;
}
```

```ts
  sameResumeRuns: number; // 同一份简历最多跑过几场
}
```

Code:

```ts
import type { CategoryShortfall, JudgedClaimLike } from './shortfall.ts';
import { categoryShortfalls } from './shortfall.ts';
import type { VerdictCounts } from './compare.ts';
import { verdictCounts } from './compare.ts';

export interface SessionFacts {
  id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  resumeHash: string;
  claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[];
  probes: number | null;
}

export const TREND_POINTS = 5;

export interface TrendPoint {
  sessionId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  judged: number;
  solid: number;
  probes: number | null;
}

export interface ResumeGroup {
  resumeHash: string;
  sessions: number;
  latestId: string;
  latestCreatedAt: string;
}

export interface HistoryOverview {
  sessions: number;
  claims: number;
  verdicts: VerdictCounts;
  categories: CategoryShortfall[];
  meanProbes: number | null;
  trend: TrendPoint[];
  trendNote: string | null;
  resumeGroups: ResumeGroup[];
  sameResumeRuns: number;
  headline: string;
}

/** 时间倒序（与库里的 ORDER BY 一致），并列时按 id 倒序，保证同一批输入永远同一顺序。 */
function newestFirst(a: SessionFacts, b: SessionFacts): number {
  if (a.createdAt !== b.createdAt) return a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : -1;
  if (a.id === b.id) return 0;
  return a.id < b.id ? 1 : -1;
}

function meanOf(values: readonly number[]): number | null {
  if (values.length === 0) return null;
  let sum = 0;
  for (const v of values) sum += v;
  return Math.round((sum / values.length) * 10) / 10;
}

export function trendPointOf(session: SessionFacts): TrendPoint {
  const counts = verdictCounts(session.claims);
  return {
    sessionId: session.id,
    createdAt: session.createdAt,
    jobTitle: session.jobTitle,
    judged: counts.judged,
    solid: counts.solid,
    probes: session.probes,
  };
}

export function overviewHeadline(overview: Omit<HistoryOverview, 'headline' | 'trendNote'>): string {
  if (overview.sessions === 0) return '还没有跑过任何一场。';
  if (overview.claims === 0) return String(overview.sessions) + ' 场都还没抽出主张。';
  if (overview.verdicts.judged === 0) {
    return String(overview.sessions) + ' 场共 ' + String(overview.claims) + ' 条主张，一条都还没判。';
  }
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push(String(overview.sessions) + ' 场共 ' + String(overview.claims) + ' 条主张');
  parts.push('已判 ' + String(overview.verdicts.judged) + ' 条里站得住 ' + String(overview.verdicts.solid) + ' 条');
  const weak = overview.verdicts.weak;
  if (weak > 0) {
    const focus = overview.categories.find((item) => item.weak > 0);
    if (focus !== undefined) parts.push('还欠 ' + String(weak) + ' 条，最集中的是「' + focus.label + '」' + String(focus.weak) + ' 条');
    else parts.push('还欠 ' + String(weak) + ' 条');
  } else {
    parts.push('没有一条欠证据');
  }
  if (overview.meanProbes !== null) parts.push('平均每场追问 ' + String(overview.meanProbes) + ' 次');
  return parts.join('，') + '。';
}

export function overviewOf(sessions: readonly SessionFacts[]): HistoryOverview {
  const ordered = [...sessions].sort(newestFirst);
  const allClaims: JudgedClaimLike[] = [];
  for (const session of ordered) for (const claim of session.claims) allClaims.push(claim);
  const probeValues: number[] = [];
  for (const session of ordered) if (session.probes !== null) probeValues.push(session.probes);
  const groups = new Map<string, ResumeGroup>();
  for (const session of ordered) {
    const found = groups.get(session.resumeHash);
    if (found === undefined) {
      groups.set(session.resumeHash, { resumeHash: session.resumeHash, sessions: 1, latestId: session.id, latestCreatedAt: session.createdAt });
    } else found.sessions += 1;
  }
  const resumeGroups = [...groups.values()];
  let sameResumeRuns = 0;
  for (const group of resumeGroups) if (group.sessions > sameResumeRuns) sameResumeRuns = group.sessions;
  // 趋势点取最近 TREND_POINTS 场，但按时间升序摆：左手边是更早的
  const recent = ordered.slice(0, TREND_POINTS).reverse();
  const partial = {
    sessions: ordered.length,
    claims: allClaims.length,
    verdicts: verdictCounts(allClaims),
    categories: categoryShortfalls(allClaims),
    meanProbes: meanOf(probeValues),
    trend: recent.map(trendPointOf),
    resumeGroups,
    sameResumeRuns,
  };
  const trendNote = partial.trend.length < 2 ? '只跑了 ' + String(partial.sessions) + ' 场，趋势要等第二场跑完才看得出来。' : null;
  return { ...partial, trendNote, headline: overviewHeadline(partial) };
}
```

```ts
export function trendLine(trend: readonly TrendPoint[]): string | null {
  if (trend.length < 2) return null;
  return '最近 ' + String(trend.length) + ' 场：站得住 ' + trend.map((p) => String(p.solid)).join(' → ') + ' 条';
}
```

```text
> 这一份是第 13 轮对话（2026-10-26 夜）的产物：把「还要做什么」定死，并回答两个问题——
```

```text
追问强度三档与改写风格三档看起来是同一类功能（都让用户拨一个滑块），落地方式却完全相反：前者进了契约指纹、
每档一套 probe 卡带；后者刻意**不进**指纹，suggest 卡带才从 4 盘涨到 12 盘。区别不在实现难易，而在它改的是哪一层——
强度改的是预算，预算写在契约里，所以它属于「谁在问、问多深」；风格改的是措辞收敛程度，属于「改得多狠」。
把两个旋钮放进同一个指纹，历史场次之间就无法比较：用户会看到「换了个风格」被界面解释成「换了一套面试官」。

### F66 · 新增一个阶段时，测试里的 purpose 分派和响应形状都要跟着改

改写风格加档位后，`packages/server/test/style.test.ts` 连续失败两次，两次原因都不是被测代码：
第一次是测试桩把 suggest 的返回写成了数组（`{ suggestions: [...] }`），而这一阶段一次只问一条主张、返回的是**单个对象**，
于是每条都报 `problem: Required; before: Required; after: Required; why: Required`；
第二次是把判定写成 `solid`——而 `SUGGESTIBLE` 只有 overstated / unsupported / contradicts / unanswerable，
凡是「站得住」的主张改写阶段整段跳过，三档自然拿不到任何建议，测试看起来像「三档没区别」。
```

```text
# ADR-0018：版本对比按文本相似度配对，不另存一份「档案」

- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-27，P11 的 C1）
- 相关：决策 D54、发现 F67、`packages/shared/src/compare.ts`、`packages/server/src/compare.ts`、`packages/shared/src/compare.ts` 的 `alignClaims`

## 背景

「改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变」是这份产品最该回答的问题，也是它区别于一次性简历体检的地方。
要回答它，必须先解决一个没有现成答案的问题：**两场会话的主张怎么配成对**。

主张没有稳定标识。每场会话都从 C1 重新编号（这是刻意的，见 F 系列关于 id 的结论），
而用户采纳改写之后，被改的那一句在原文里已经不存在了——文本变了，id 也变了，
两个都不适合拿来当配对依据。

## 决策

1. **按主张文本的相似度配对**，不引入外部标识：`packages/shared/src/compare.ts` 的
   `alignClaims(prev, next, threshold = DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD)` 用 bigram Jaccard 相似度
   全对打分，按分数降序贪心一对一，剩下没配上的进 `added` / `removed`。
   `DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD = 0.34` 是拿真实简历试出来的：换说法落在 0.45–0.65，
   同主语不同事约 0.2，毫不相干约 0。
2. **配对结果按「这一场」的顺序重排**，不按上一场。报告是新简历的读法，从上往下读，
   每一条旁边说一下它上次是什么；按上一场排会让读者反复回跳。
3. **只存一条版本链，不存「档案」表**。`session.parent_session_id`（schema v10）记「这一版是从哪一场改出来的」，
   对比时现算。刻意**不加外键**：删掉上一版不该把这一版带走，此时接口回 `parentMissing` 一档，
   界面说「上一版不在了」，而不是假装这是第一版。
4. **判定位移复用短板排序**：`verdictShift` 用 `shortfall.ts` 的 `WEAKNESS_RANK`
   （solid 0 / overstated 1 / unsupported 2 / contradicts 3 / unanswerable 4）比大小，
   任一为 `null` 记 `unjudged`。**不新写一套「好坏」判断**——报告里的短板排序和版本对比
   必须对同一件事给出同一个答案（与 D47 同族）。
5. **结论只算一次**：`compareHeadlin
…（此处省略 456 字符，原始长度 1656）
```

```text
195:| D50 | 「改前改后对照」的行由 `packages/shared/src/revise.ts` 的 `sideBySide` 产出，报告与界面都读它；**没采纳过就整节不出现**（章节编号跟着回退） | 同一份结论在报告与界面各算一遍，失败方式不是崩溃而是「两个都对但不一样」（F63，与 D47 同族）。「没采纳过」时那种「对照」只是把同一份原文抄两遍，摆出来会让人以为简历已经改过了；所以 `renderReport` / `renderReportHtml` 都收一个 `applied`，为空就跳过这一节，复现方式的编号也从七退回六 |
196:| D51 | 对照表只摆**改动行 + 上下文行**，不做字符级 diff | 中文改写常常是整句重写，字符级 diff 会碎成一堆无意义的片段，反而看不出「改成了什么」。读者真正缺的信息是「这句改动落在简历的什么位置」，所以把改动之间没动过的原文也摆进来当上下文行（F62）。代价是左右两列不逐字符对齐——这是刻意的：它要服务的是「读得懂」，不是「逐字对齐」 |
197:| D52 | 技术文档**不再让每章强制另起一页** | 30 页是赛题硬上限，而 `h1.section { break-before: page }` 会把每章末尾那半页空白也算进预算：31 页里有一整页只有 230 个字符。改成章间留白（9mm）+ 一条细分隔线之后，同一份内容 31 → 28 页，余量从 0 变成 2 页。代价是章标题可能出现在页面中部——对一份要被检索、被打开展示的技术文档来说，这个代价远小于「为了省几行去删掉一条论证」（见 F64） |
198:| D53 | 改写风格三档**不进契约指纹**，只随 `session.meta.style` 落库 | 契约指纹回答的是「谁在问」，风格回答的是「改得多狠」——两个旋钮混进同一个指纹，用户会看到「换了个风格」被界面解释成「换了一套面试官」，历史场次之间就此不可比（追问强度相反：它改的是预算，预算写在契约里，所以它进指纹，见 D46）。代价是档位进提示词＝换档换指纹＝suggest 卡带从 4 盘涨到 12 盘；三条红线（不加新数字、不把参与写成主导、before 必须锚得回）在任何档位都不放宽，`bold` 档由 `newNumbersIn` 在代码里兜底。见 ADR-0017、F65 |
```

Key design points for C2:

5. 趋势不足两场就不画、时间升序；空库也要给一句话
6. resume_hash 分组给出「同一份简历跑过 N 场」，为 P12 成长档案留接口

```text
> 用法：从仓库根目录自上而下走一遍，每一项都能勾掉才提交。
> 标 ⛔ 的是**只有参赛者本人能决定**的事项，AI 无法代办。

## 一、赛题要求的五项提交物

| # | 要求 | 现状 | 位置 / 命令 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | GitHub / Gitee 公开仓库，**细粒度 commit**（禁止一次性 initial commit） | ✅ 250 余次提交，每次对应一次具体开发动作 | 见第二节「建远端」 |
| 2 | **≥3 个核心功能迭代的完整 Prompt 链**（提问 → AI 建议 → 如何引导修 Bug） | ✅ 自动导出，34 份文件（其中 30 份分页快照，约 14 MB） | `npm run export:prompt` → `docs/prompt-chain/` |

**为什么是 ⛔**：仓库地址与账号归属只有参赛者能决定，AI 拿不到也不该代填。
```

- 说明：Add rerun and overview rows to README features
- 代码：
```js
const fs = await import('node:fs');
const p = 'D:/CZB/README.md';
let t = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8');
const anchor = '| 带走 | 报告三种形态：Markdown / 可打印 HTML（另存为 PDF）/ Word；改完的简历另外三种：`.txt` / 导出 PDF / 导出 Word |';
if (!t.includes(anchor)) throw new Error('missing anchor');
const add = anchor + '\n| 复跑 | **改完再跑一遍**：采纳改写后点「改完再跑一遍」，它拿改完的简历重开一场并记下父会话，逐条告诉你哪些主张的判定变好了（按文本相似度把两场的主张配上对，配不上的单独摆出来）；两份报告开头也会多一节「比上次」 |\n| 回顾 | **历史仪表盘**：历史栏顶上一句话结论（几场、多少条主张、站得住多少、最欠证据的类别、平均每场追问几次），加最近 5 场的趋势柱与「同一份简历跑过几场」——跑到第三场之后就靠它看出是在变好还是在打转 |';
t = t.replace(anchor, add);
fs.writeFileSync(p, t);
console.log('ok');

```

```text
| 拆解 | 抽出「可被追问的主张」，每条锚定简历原文 |
| 审阅 | **岗位要求对照**：贴了 JD 就先把它读成一张要求清单（逐份抽要求 → 跨份归并 → 与你的主张逐条对照），分成「答得上 / 答得含糊 / 简历里没写」三组；前两组会被拿去追问，一条追问同时挂 JD 原文片段与简历片段**两处锚点**，点任一处右栏就聚焦到那一段 |
| 组庭 | 五位预设面试官（技术 / HR / 业务主管 / 压力 / 领域专家），也可以在**面试官工作室**里自己配一位：一句话描述 → 补成意图契约，没有 API Key 就用本地规则生成；契约、指纹与渲染出的 System Prompt 都能摊开看 |
| 对抗 | 多面试官并发追问，你回答，它继续追问 |
| 判定 | 每条主张：站得住 / 表述过度 / 证据缺失 / 与原文矛盾 / 无法回答，并写明「为什么这样判」 |
| 反推 | 定位到原文片段的简历修改建议 |
| 短板 | 同一批判定换个角度：按主张类别聚合，先排欠得最多的那一类，再列出最该补的 3 处，点一下跳回简历原文 |
| 强度 | **追问强度**三档：温和 / 常规 / 挑刺。换的是预算（每主张 2 / 3 / 4 轮、全场 40 / 60 / 80 次）不是人设，档位随会话留档 |
| 风格 | **改写风格**三档：保守（只做减法）/ 常规 / 敢改（整句重写）。只调措辞收敛程度，三条红线不动：不加简历里没有的数字、不把参与写成主导、改前原文必须锚得回原文 |
| 补证据 | 任何一条主张上可以补一句真话当作新证据；采纳时它作为新的一行进简历，原文一个字不动 |
| 反问 | 跑完给你几句能反问 HR 的话，每句都必须锚回简历原话或 JD 原文，锚不回的当场丢弃；回答明确标注是沙盘模拟 |
| 改稿 | 勾选想采纳的建议 → **确认后自动改简历**，右栏直接换成改完的版本；改动段标绿，改不动的（引文在原简历里锚不住）会说明理由。点「并排看改动」还能逐条对照改前改后，并看到它们落在简历的什么位置 |
| 带走 | 报告三种形态：Markdown / 可打印 HTML（另存为 PDF）/ Word；改完的简历另外三种：`.txt` / 导出 PDF / 导出 Word
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I need to design C3: single-file HTML bundle including report + timeline + resume original + revisions + counters, no external resources.

Key insight: report-html.ts already produces a self-contained HTML with inline styles and no external links. C3 basically needs to add extra sections: 简历原文 full text (with highlight of revisions?), 时间线 (event timeline), 本版改动 (already in sideSection), 反问 (counterSection already). Report already includes claims, requirements, transcript, counters, advice, side-by-side, repro.

So C3 = a "bundle" variant that adds:
1. 完整简历原文 (full text, not just quotes)
2. 过程时间线 (the event stream / stage timeline) — this is the unique addition: it's the "AI 协同过程" evidence
3. maybe 用法/元信息 header

Let me check what's already in report: claims + verdicts + requirements + transcript + counters + advice + side + repro. Missing: full resume text, and the event timeline.

So the plan: `GET /api/sessions/:id/bundle.html` with `Content-Disposition: attachment`, reusing renderReportHtml + two extra sections. But careful: "不许出现 http:// / https:// 外链" — the report may include resume email etc. Let me check if existing report has any links. Probably not.

Design decision: implement `renderBundleHtml(view, options)` in a new file `packages/server/src/bundle.ts` that calls renderReportHtml and injects extra sections? That's hacky. Better: extend renderReportHtml with an option `bundle?: boolean` that adds sections (完整简历 + 时间线) and a "本文件是单文件打包" note. But the ordering/renumber gets complicated.

Cleaner: add two new sections to the bundle renderer only, placed after repro section (as appendix, numbered 八/九 or unnumbered appendix). Actually appendix sections "附录一、简历原文" "附录二、过程时间线" avoids messing up renumbering.

Let me look at how events/timeline data looks: SessionView.events = EventRow[] with {seq, kind, payload, createdAt}. There's a stage concept. Let me check what kinds exist.

Also need to verify no external URLs: the bundle must not reference http(s). I'll add a test asserting no 'http://' or 'https://' in output... but resume text may contain email/links. Hmm, the requirement is about *resources* (no external stylesheet/script/img). Better test: no `<link`, no `src=`, no `@import`, no `url(http`. Actually a resume could contain "https://github.com/..." as text, which is fine (it's content, not resource). But the design doc says "不许出现 http:// / https:// 外链（评委断网也要能打开）" — intent is resources.

I'll write the check as: no `<link `, no `src="http`, no `@import`, no `url(http`. And also verify no external `<a href="http` maybe convert to plain text? Actually links to external are fine to open but they are "外链". Let me be strict but sensible: check for resource references; and for `href="http` too — since the report doesn't produce them, and resume text is escaped as text, not links. So a strict check `/(https?:\/\/)/` would fail only if resume text contains a URL. In demo resume there's an email `zhangming@example.com` but no http. Test uses demo resume so strict check passes. But a real user resume with a GitHub link would then... the check is only in the test, not runtime. But better make runtime also safe: it's just text.

Hmm — but if we make it an attachment "打包分享", the resume text containing a URL as text is fine.

Let me decide the acceptance: 
- output starts with `<!doctype html>`, contains `<style>` inline, no `<link`, no `<script src=`, no `@import`, no `url(http`.
- size sane (< 300 KB).
- Content-Disposition attachment with filename.
- contains 简历原文 full text and 时间线.

Now what about the frontend button "打包分享" in RevisionBar or header. Header already has 报告 .md / PDF / Word. Add 打包(.html)? Better in header as fourth button? The design says 右栏加「打包分享」按钮. RevisionBar is 采纳改写 with revised state. But bundle applies to any finished session. So put in header next to 报告 buttons: `报告 打包`.

Hmm, but RevisionBar is "采纳改写" — the bundle isn't about revised. Let me put in header: `报告 .md` `报告 PDF` `报告 Word` `打包分享`. Use data-shot="report-bundle".

Let me check App.tsx header region and api.ts reportUrl functions.

Also need timeline data: does SessionView include events with stage? Let me check state.ts STAGE_LABEL and event kinds.

Let me look at the event kinds used.

```text
export function esc(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
}

const NUMERALS = '一二三四五六七八九';
const H2 = '## ';

/** 只认 '## 一、xxx' 这种二级标题：h3 的小节不编号，{ } 里的内容也不动 */
function bumpNumeral(text: string): string {
  if (!text.startsWith(H2)) return text;
  const body = text.slice(H2.length);
  const at = NUMERALS.indexOf(body.slice(0, 1));
  if (at < 0) return text;
  return H2 + NUMERALS.charAt(at + 1) + body.slice(1);
}

/** 有「比上次」时后面所有章节编号整体 +1：两种渲染器共用同一条规则，免得两边章节号对不上 */
function renumber(text: string, on: boolean): string {
  return on && text.startsWith('## ') ? bumpNumeral(text) : text;
}

/**
 * 「比上次」一节（HTML 版）：与 Markdown 报告同一句话、同一份数据。
 * 三种情况分得开——是第一版、上一版被删、上一版没跑完——空着一节会被当成功能坏了。
 */
function compareSection(compare: CompareView): string[] {
  if (compare.first) return [];
  const out: string[] = ['<h2>比上次</h2>'];

```

```text
  };
```

```text
function resumeSection(view: SessionView, applied: readonly Revision[]): string[] {
  const out: string[] = ['<h2>附二、简历原文' + (applied.length > 0 ? '（含本版改动）' : '') + '</h2>'];
  if (applied.length === 0) {
    out.push('<pre class="text">' + esc(view.session.resumeText) + '</pre>');

  const revised = applyRevisions(view.session.resumeText, applied);

    + '没有标绿的原文一个字都没有动。</p>');
  out.push('<pre class="text">' + highlightText(view.session.revisedText ?? view.session.resumeText, spans) + '</pre>');
  return out;
}
```

```text
/**
 * 测试用的"脚本化模型"：不发网络请求，按调用次序返回预先准备好的文本。
 *
 * 为什么值得单独存在：多 Agent 流水线的测试痛点是"每个 Agent 都要一份假数据"，
 * 用脚本化客户端之后，测试写的是"第 1 次调用返回什么"，而不是"怎么 mock fetch"。
 * 它同时也是离线演示的录制工具——record 模式包上它，就能在没有任何 API Key 的机器上
 * 生成一整套完整卡带。
 */

import type { LlmClient, LlmRequest, LlmStreamEvent } from '../src/llm/types.ts';

export type ScriptedHandler = (req: LlmRequest, index: number) => string | Error;

export interface ScriptedClient extends LlmClient {
  readonly calls: LlmRequest[];
  /** 每次调用的返回值，便于断言"到底调了几次、按什么顺序" */
  countPurpose(purpose: string): number;
}

const USAGE = { inputTokens: 10, outputTokens: 20, cacheReadTokens: 0, reasoningTokens: 0 };

export function createScriptedClient(handler: ScriptedHandler): ScriptedClient {
  const calls: LlmRequest[] = [];
  function take(req: LlmRequest): string {
    calls.push(req);
    const out = handler(req, calls.length - 1);
    if (out instanceof Error) throw out;
    return out;
  }
  const client: LlmClient = {
    mode: 'live',
    async complete(req: LlmRequest) {
      const text = take(req);
      return { text, usage: USAGE, digest: 'scripted-' + String(calls.length), replayed: false, elapsedMs: 0 };
    },
    async *stream(req: LlmReques
…（此处省略 2914 字符，原始长度 4114）
```

```text
export function fetchInterviewers(): Promise<InterviewerLibrary> {
  return getJson<InterviewerLibrary>('/api/interviewers');
}

export function draftInterviewer(input: DraftInput): Promise<DraftResult> {
  return postJson<DraftResult>('/api/interviewers/draft', input);
}

export function saveInterviewer(input: {
  draft: InterviewerDraft;
  id?: string;
  source?: string;
}): Promise<{ ok: boolean; interviewer: InterviewerEntry }> {
  return postJson<{ ok: boolean; interviewer: InterviewerEntry }>('/api/interviewers', input);
}

export async function deleteInterviewer(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await fetch('/api/interviewers/' + encodeURIComponent(id), { method: 'DELETE' });
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(await readError(res), res.status);
}

async function postJson<T>(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(path, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(await readError(res), res.status);
  return (await res.json()) as T;
}
```

```text
# ADR-0019：历史仪表盘只做聚合，不引入新判定

- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-27，P11 的 C2）
- 相关：决策 D55、发现 F69/F70、`packages/shared/src/overview.ts`、`packages/server/src/overview.ts`、`packages/web/src/overview.ts`

## 背景

历史栏原本只是「一列场次」。跑到第三场之后，这一列就失效了：用户看不出自己是在变好还是在打转，
而「变没变好」恰恰是这份产品最该回答的问题（与 C1 同一个动机，只是 C1 比两场、C2 比很多场）。

难点不在界面，在于**「这些数字由谁算」**。报告里已经有「站得住 N 条」、短板里已经有「最该补的类别」，
如果仪表盘自己再算一遍，两处迟早会对同一份数据给出不同的数字——这不是崩溃，是更难查的那种失败（F27 / D47 同族）。

## 决策

1. **聚合是一个纯函数，在 shared 里**：`packages/shared/src/overview.ts` 的 `overviewOf(sessions: SessionFacts[])`。
   它只接收数据、不读库、不调模型、不看时间，因此可以被单测钉死，也能被报告或其它界面复用。
   服务端 `packages/server/src/overview.ts` 只做一件事：读库把 `SessionFacts` 攒出来，交给它。
2. **判定分布与类别短板一律复用现成实现**：`verdictCounts`（`compare.ts`）与 `categoryShortfalls`（`shortfall.ts`）。
   **不新写第二套「哪个判定更差」的排序**。仪表盘说「最集中的是技能掌握」，报告里那一条就必须也是技能掌握。
3. **追问数取不到就是 `null`，不猜 0**：`SessionFacts.probes` 允许为 `null`，`meanOf` 只在非 `null` 的场次上求平均。
   猜 0 会让「还没跑完的场次」把平均值往下拽，而界面没有任何办法把这件事说出来。
4. **窗口上限 200 场，且这是性能边界不是分页**：`OVERVIEW_WINDOW = 200`。
   不设上限的话，跑到几百场之后每次打开页面都要把每一场的主张与追问各读一遍。
   接口照样收 `?limit=`，但界面上没有「翻页」——仪表盘回答的是「最近这一阵子怎么样」。
5. **趋势不足两场就不画**：`trendLine` 在少于两个点时返回 `null`，界面上整块趋势不出现，
   换一句「只跑了 N 场，趋势要等第二场跑完才看得出来。」而不是画一根孤零零的柱子假装有趋势。
   趋势点**时间升序**（`
…（此处省略 639 字符，原始长度 1839）
```

```text
**真问题**

- 「同一件事算两次」的失败不是崩溃，是两个都对但不一样（F69）。仪表盘完全可以自己 `reduce` 一遍判定分布，
  但哪天「欠证据算不算欠」这种口径一改，它就和报告分叉。处置是直接 import 现成实现，
  并且自己在 shared 里**不持有任何「哪个判定更差」的知识**（排序仍只在 `WEAKNESS_RANK` 一处）。
- 第一版把「空库」与「还没取到」混成一种状态（都返回 `null`），界面每一处都要写三元判断，
  刷新时还会闪一下「还没有
```

```js
## 背景

能导出的东西一直是四份：报告 md / html / doc，简历 txt / html / doc。自己带走够用，

而这个产品最需要被传播的那一句话（「你简历里这句话，撑不住」）恰恰是在报告里的。

于是要一份「打包版」：一个文件，双击就看完整场。

## 决策

   整段塞进打包文件，再在后面接两节附录。理由与 F63 同族：同一份事实两套渲染，
   迟早会长成两个版本——报告里说「变好 4 条」，打包里说「变好 3 条」，用户没法判断哪个是真的。
2. **「不许出现 `http(s)://`」是一条可执行的断言，不是文档里的一句话**。
   收件人的机器可能
```
